including optional and host build/proc-macro dependencies, not a claim that
all listed code is linked into the final WASM. Fixture-only native generator
dependencies are not represented as browser runtime dependencies.

  and Zlib OR MIT OR Apache-2.0 alternatives.

Original license alternatives/notices are retained below even where another
permitted alternative was selected. Identical license text is consolidated,
with every applicable package/file attribution listed before its text.

The Rust 1.99.1 standard library is MIT OR Apache-2.0 with additional
per-file/dependency notices. MIT is selected where permitted. The installed
COPYRIGHT-library.html is transcribed below as text, retaining attributions
and exceptions and consolidating identical preformatted notice blocks.
This is a conservative multi-platform library notice inventory, including
platform code not necessarily present in wasm32 with panic=abort. It is not
a claim that every listed optional/system component is redistributed.
Rust/LLVM exceptions and other terms remain attached to their own code.

Cached crate license/copyright files are reproduced below. Where a crate
omitted the text, the upstream file was recovered at the exact revision in
its .cargo_vcs_info.json; those four source URLs are recorded explicitly.
